# In the organization, not on APIs.io

Repositories in the `api-evangelist` GitHub organization that are named for a provider but have no live provider page on APIs.io as of 2026-11-15. They are not in the index until a page exists. Infrastructure repositories (the sites, tools and data that run the network), investors (rendered under apis.io/vcs/) and retired or delisted companies are excluded here and from the index.

## Profiled, but no live page (3,137)

Artifacts exist in the profile store or a page was built once, but the page is missing or now redirects.
